# Coordination: antigravity agent <-> Claude sessions

The user runs an antigravity agent (`e4955d7c-2bf7-49e0-85af-59f9f790cf6b`) in this same folder alongside several Claude
Code sessions. Claude sessions cannot message antigravity directly, so this file is the shared channel.

**Every agent (Claude or antigravity): read this file and `git status` / `git diff` before starting or resuming work.**
Append notes at the bottom (newest last), with a date, your name, what you are changing and which files. Don't rewrite
other entries.

## Messaging the Claude sessions directly

Antigravity can message a Claude Code session directly, and the Claude session replies through the same channel (it
answers to the sender address the message arrives with). Message the **main coordinating session** first:

- **Main session**: "Game design specification", session id `local_05e729b0-b796-447c-8947-368fc5bb1846`. It routes
  work and knows who owns what.
- Area owners, if you need one directly: Weapons `local_6690022c-6456-4e85-a202-232e0210d47f`, Environment (city)
  `local_e88e8643-bca3-44aa-81d6-c8858873ba94`, Engine (HUD/menus) `local_895e82af-01f2-4d92-a95b-ff9536874a50`, Mech
  `local_8798fcb8-3331-48cd-93e8-7072f330aeef`, Missions (section) `local_cf0753b6-a39b-4dbf-ba55-f98caa0d79b5`,
  Pilots (heads) `local_eeb1cceb-e20b-48e6-adb3-a8cdce5a4d20`.

Say in the first line what the message is about. This file stays the written record: note the files you change here too.

Paused work is listed in `docs/paused/` and resumes around midnight. Baseline commit: `41f65b7`; everything since is
uncommitted.

## Rules that matter for everyone
- `AGENTS.md`: the networking contract (enforced by `tests/test_net_contract.gd`), headless testing, and the **GPU lock**
  (`C:\Users\alexa\.claude\gpu-lock\gpu_lock.ps1`): never open a visible Godot window or run ComfyUI outside the lock.
  Screenshots go through `Invoke-HiddenDesktop` (see `docs/testing.md`).
- Run `.\test_runner.ps1 -TestAll` after changes; keep it green.
- Don't commit unless the user asks.

- 2026-11-21, Claude "Mech" (meshes) session, **for antigravity**: the accent-colour surface has landed. Low-poly mech
  meshes now have up to three surfaces named "matte", "glow", "accent" (`MeshKit.SURFACES`); the formerly orange faces are
  in "accent". `tests/test_geometry_audit.gd` (yours, I did not edit it) now reports open edges on MechMeshes torso (56),
  back_plate (6) and thigh (14). The meshes are still closed: `_check_mesh_winding` declares `var edge_counts := {}` inside
  `for s in mesh.get_surface_count()`, so an edge shared by an accent face and a matte face is only seen from one side.
  Fix: declare `edge_counts` once before the surface loop and run the open-edge tally (the `for k in edge_counts` block)
  once after it, so half-edges are matched across all surfaces of the mesh. Face counts and geometry are unchanged.
  Recolour API: `MechModel.set_accent(color)`, `MechMeshes.apply_accent(node, color)`.

- 2026-11-22, Claude strafe-gait subagent: **hip twist landed** (files as listed above, plus a two-line fix in
  `tests/test_ring_step.gd` (Mech session's): its pelvis-hip and strafe foot-gap checks now measure in the legs' frame,
  `MechModel.twist_basis(rig.leg_yaw)`). `MechRig.leg_yaw` (rad, + = legs toward the body's right; `twist_max` 90 deg,
  `twist_rate` 270 deg/s; reversed gait beyond ~100 deg; back to 0 when slow or airborne). Strafing at 7.2 m/s: 11.0 ->
  2.6 steps/s, mean step 1.32 -> 5.7 m. **For antigravity (audio), a hook you may use in `scripts/audio/mech_audio.gd`:**
  `Gait` now has `signal stepped(foot: int, info: Dictionary)`, emitted when a step lands, info =
  `{kind, length, weight, pos}`; kind is `"stride"` (walking step), `"correction"` (short re-placing step while moving),
  `"settle"` (re-plant at a standstill) or `"land"` (touchdown re-plant); `length` m, `weight` = length / step_length
  (0..1). The same info stays on the foot as `feet[i]["last"]`, and `feet[i]["kind"]` / `["length"]` (planned) are set
  when a foot lifts off, so the servo-on-lift sound can be filtered too. E.g. play `mech.step.*` only for `stride` (or
  scale its volume by `weight`), skip `land` (the landing sound covers it). Connect via `_rig.gait.stepped`. I did not
  edit `scripts/audio/`.

- 2026-11-22, Weapons session subagent (Claude): **landed: mission assets (the Defend relay as a combat target).** New `scripts/combat/asset_target.gd` (`AssetTarget extends CombatTarget`), `TestDriveCombat.add_asset(id, node, radius := 2.5, height := 6.0) -> AssetTarget` / `remove_asset(id)` / `asset(id)`; tests `test_asset_target`, `test_net_asset_target` (2), `test_asset_mission`; `docs/design/weapons.md` §5.1 "Mission assets" + status row. No edit to repair_drone / combat_net / combat_world, nothing outside scripts/combat/. **Contract (Engine / Missions):** call `add_asset` on EVERY machine with the objective id (idempotent for the same id + node; a new node replaces it); target id `"asset_<id>"`, team `"player"`, `owner_peer` 0; on the host each applied hit (enemy fire decided there, a client's friendly-fire shot via the existing `combat.hit`) emits `asset_damaged(asset_id, amount, src)` and calls `td.mission.damage_asset(id, amount)` in hit points (looked up per hit, duck-typed); the target subtracts nothing; push the host's HP with `set_hp(hp, max_hp)` on every machine; `hp_fraction()`; `is_hittable()` false once hp <= 0 (true before the first set_hp). Hit spheres stack up the node's local +Y from its origin, first sphere at mid-height. A `CollisionObject3D` node (MissionWorld's relay body) becomes the target's excluded body (member `mech`, read by `CombatWorld._body_excludes`): shots reach the spheres, no wall scorch on it. Repair drone never heals it. Verified with Engine's live `_sync_asset_targets`: lethal enemy-team hit -> `asset_hp` 0 -> "lost" / objective_failed (`test_asset_mission`); `test_mission_objectives` still passes. **For antigravity (enemy AI, nothing required today):** `_acquire_target` already takes it (team "player", hittable, on deck, LOS; aims at the first sphere); a catalog turret with no player around acquires and fires at it (tested, physics off and on with a relay-sized 4.2 x 5.4 x 4.2 box seen face-on). Two things to know: (1) `_check_los` does not exclude the target's own collision body and accepts a stop within 2.6 m of the aim centre; the relay box's half-width is 2.1 m face-on but up to ~3.0 m across a corner and more from a rooftop eye, so LOS to the relay can fail from diagonal / high angles. One-line fix if wanted: in `_check_los`, exclude the target's body (`_ray_query.exclude = [t.mech.get_rid()]` when `"mech" in t and t.mech is CollisionObject3D`), or accept a stop inside the target's bounding sphere. (2) `EnemySquad.deck_awake` counts every hittable team-"player" target, so a live relay keeps its deck's enemies awake with no player on it (arguably right for Defend; it costs AI frame budget). Also `bot_metrics` counts enemy damage on the relay as damage to "player".

(1) **Loop voices** (`scripts/audio/audio_director.gd`): real: `start_loop` refused every loop past 8 with a silent 0 (repro in `tests/test_audio.gd` `_test_loop_voices`: 11 loops of a busy machine, `slot3, slot4, lock` dropped). Fix: `POOL_LOOPS` 8 -> 24 (loops were already one node each, so no pool to grow) and past the cap `_loop_victim` steals: world loops (3D, not SFX_Player) before local ones, farthest from the listener first; a world request needs `LOOP_STEAL_MARGIN_M` (5 m) over the farthest world loop; a local loop is never stolen; refusals counted (`loops_refused`, `loops_stolen`, both Net.LOCAL) and recorded (`loop_refused`). - 2026-11-25, Mech session subagent (gait phase): **landed (uncommitted).** Playtest 'legs get in sync, hopping'. Cause: `Gait` had no phase coupling (each foot steps on its own distance trigger; the only rule was 'not while the other swings' at a walk), so any common start (from rest, landing, stop/start, turn) left the feet in step, and with `overlap_k` 0 (Bulwark: next step once the other is halfway down) + `reach_err` 2.5 they stayed ~0.04 cycle apart = a hop. `scripts/mech/gait.gd`: phase lock (foot's turn when the other is half a cycle on and it is the one further on; never lifts over the other's swing unless the cycle has a flight and that one is nearly down; alternate leader; turning: per-foot lead from each hip's travel; reach emergency kept, marked `forced`; crowding overlap only while the hips swivel or feet close in a flight/shuffle); new LOCAL vars `_last_lift` `_prev_homes`. No rig numbers changed. New `tests/test_gait_phase.gd` (real rig both chassis + curbs + jittery puppet; fails on the old gait). Bulwark walk both-in-swing 22% -> 0%, phase offset mean err 0.23 -> 0.02.

Cause: the ramp parapet collision boxes were tilted to the slope, so their end face leaned 0.4 m out past the drawn foot; the capsule sank into that invisible corner and Godot's capsule-box contacts then blocked every move_and_slide. The 1.3 m end blocks did the same once the overhang was gone (a box corner whose top is inside the capsule's straight side). Fix, collision only (meshes untouched, shape counts unchanged): `CityDistrict._ramp_collision` parapets are now convex prisms ending square to the deck, flush with the drawn ends (SEAM overlap only between pieces, `_parapet_points`); end blocks are `END_GUARD` 6.5 m tall (invisible post above the drawn 1.3 m). New tests/test_ramp_stuck.gd (seeds 9/1/2/4/6, every ramp end corner, Strider + Bulwark: 20 pinned before, 0 after). Collision hash pins re-captured (same counts) in tests/test_city_variety.gd and tests/test_city_farm_walls.gd.

- 2026-11-26, Engine (sub-agent): `MissionBot._plan_local` capsule-fit fallback (agreed with Missions / Environment). The usual pick is unchanged: within STAND_IN x r, roomy. Only when it finds nothing does the bot take any reachable point inside the objective's own radius (a defend: 30 m) that clears body_r + FIT_PAD (0.6 m) all round, nearest the goal point. `_roomy(p, up, dist)` takes an optional reach; new stat `local_fits`. So a Strider's stand points don't move. Bulwark seed 22: d1_p now reached (stand point x -119 s -263, 18 m from the relay) and done at 244.7 s, relay min 51 %. The mission still runs out at 950 s: the deck 2 sweep took 600 s with 5 deaths; damage taken: gunships 2177, turret_sniper 1805. Passing: bot_obj_* (all 4), test_net_bot_objectives, test_bot_depot, test_net_contract.
